 THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226 AND
227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO ISSUE WRIT
OF CERTIORARI QUASHING THE INELIGIBLE VOTER LIST OF THE
7
TH RESPONDENT SOCIETY VIDE ANNEXURE-A PREPARED BY THE
7
TH RESPONDENT SOCIETY, IN SO FARI AS PETITIONER IS
CONCERNED.
 THESE WRIT PETITIONS, COMING ON FOR ORDERS, THIS
DAY, ORDER WAS MADE THEREIN AS UNDER:
CORAM: HON'BLE MR JUSTICE SURAJ GOVINDARAJ
ORAL ORDER
1. In WP No.6076/2025 and WP No.6149/2025,
Sri.B.E.Shreedhar., learned counsel is directed to
accept notice for respondent No.7.
2. In WP No.6082/2025 and WP No.6093/2025,
Sri.B.E.Shreedhar., learned counsel is directed to
accept notice for respondent No.4.
3. This Court by way of various interim orders had
permitted the petitioners who were declared to be
ineligible voters to cast their vote in the elections of
the Society, subject to the result of the writ petition
and that the votes cast by the petitioners shall be
kept in separate ballot boxes.

 - 246 -
NC: 2025:KHC:11784
WP No. 6074 of 2025
C/W WP No. 4824 of 2025
WP No. 6038 of 2025
AND 4 OTHERS
4. A Coordinate Bench of this Court in the case of
H.S.Raju vs. State of Karnataka & others1 has
after considering the matter in detail come to a
categorical conclusion that any irregularity in the
conduct of election a petition under Article 226 of the
Constitution of India would not be the proper remedy
any dispute as regards the eligibility or ineligibility
and the deficiencies in finalization of final electoral
list, could only be considered in a dispute raised as
regard the election under Section 70 (2) of the
Karnataka Co-operative Societies Act, 1959 after the
announcement of the results by counting even the
votes cast by the petitioners.
5. The said judgment not having been challenged and
having attained finality, the fact being more or less
similar would equally apply to the present matter.
6. In that view of the matter, I pass the following:
1
 W.P.No.8502/2022 dated 7.6.2022

 - 247 -
NC: 2025:KHC:11784
WP No. 6074 of 2025
C/W WP No. 4824 of 2025
WP No. 6038 of 2025
AND 4 OTHERS
ORDER
i) The Writ Petitions are disposed of.
ii) The Returning Officer in each of the above
matters is directed to announce the result
within seven days of the receipt of a copy of
this order by taking into account the votes cast
by the petitioners in each of the above matters.
iii) In the event of any proceeding being filed in
relation to the results of the election under
Section 70 (2) of the Karnataka Co-operative
Societies Act, 1959, all the contentions raised
herein and any that may be raised are left open
for consideration in accordance with the
applicable law, this court has not expressed any
opinion on those issues.
iv) The votes cast by the respective petitioners
which had been directed to be kept in separate
box shall continue to be retained in a separate

 - 248 -
NC: 2025:KHC:11784
WP No. 6074 of 2025
C/W WP No. 4824 of 2025
WP No. 6038 of 2025
AND 4 OTHERS
box until the disposal of the dispute if any
raised within the time frame prescribed.
Sd/-
(SURAJ GOVINDARAJ)
JUDGE
SR
